allow-lan: true
mode: Rule
log-level: info
external-controller: 127.0.0.1:9090
proxies:
  - {name: 未知 VMESS-01 | free-nodes, server: 192.0.2.1, port: 1, type: vmess, uuid: 6c6a0625-ac3f-4bd8-9cc8-0545e4e11409, alterId: 0, cipher: auto, tls: false, skip-cert-verify: false}
proxy-groups:
  - name: 🚀 节点选择
    type: select
    proxies:
      - ♻️ 自动选择
      - 🇭🇰 香港节点
      - 🇺🇲 美国节点
      - ☑️ 手动切换
      - DIRECT
  - name: ☑️ 手动切换
    type: select
    proxies:
      - 未知 VMESS-01 | free-nodes
  - name: ♻️ 自动选择
    type: url-test
    url: http://www.gstatic.com/generate_204
    interval: 300
    tolerance: 50
    proxies:
      - 未知 VMESS-01 | free-nodes

    type: select
    proxies:
